# Flow catalogue

> Generated by `scripts/build-flow-catalogue.mjs`. Do not edit by hand — run
> `npm run catalogue`. `npm test` fails if this file has drifted from the definitions.

Every flow exported from the tenant: **36 definitions**. For each one, read out of
its own definition — what triggers it, what it accepts, what it touches, what it returns, and
its full structure.

**Purpose is the one thing a definition cannot state.** It is cited from the portal wiring specification, the
contract register, or the adoption ledger (the function each kept flow serves). Where none states it, the flow is marked *no stated
purpose* rather than given an invented one — an invented purpose is worse than an absent one,
because it reads as knowledge.
